# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-27)

## Corpus Check
- 7187 files · ~17,275,677 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 535 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 401, (none) 41, .css 16)

## Summary
- 71846 nodes · 211140 edges · 1615 communities (1455 shown, 160 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8787 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `c8105d7c`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
